# Licencias de terceros

Jaiba se distribuye bajo la licencia Apache-2.0 (ver [`LICENSE`](LICENSE)). Los
binarios publicados (`jaiba` para Linux y Windows, la imagen Docker y el
instalador de escritorio) incluyen software de terceros. Cada componente
conserva su propia licencia, cuyo texto se reproduce abajo.

Este archivo se genera con `python3 scripts/third-party-licenses.py`; no lo
edites a mano. El CI falla si queda desactualizado respecto a `Cargo.lock`,
`apps/jaiba-ui/src-tauri/Cargo.lock` o `apps/jaiba-ui/package-lock.json`.

## Notas

- **Oracle Instant Client**: no se redistribuye. `jaiba oracle-client install`
  lo descarga de oracle.com y su uso queda sujeto a la licencia de Oracle.
- **OpenSSL** (solo Linux): el binario usa la `libssl` del sistema; no se incluye.
- **SQLite** va incluido y es de dominio público
  (<https://sqlite.org/copyright.html>); no tiene texto de licencia que reproducir.
- Las bibliotecas del sistema que usa el escritorio en Linux (GTK, WebKitGTK)
  se enlazan dinámicamente y no se incluyen.
- Crates bajo MPL-2.0, usados sin modificar; su código fuente está en crates.io: [`cssparser 0.36.0`](https://crates.io/crates/cssparser/0.36.0), [`cssparser-macros 0.6.1`](https://crates.io/crates/cssparser-macros/0.6.1), [`dtoa-short 0.3.5`](https://crates.io/crates/dtoa-short/0.3.5), [`option-ext 0.2.0`](https://crates.io/crates/option-ext/0.2.0), [`selectors 0.36.1`](https://crates.io/crates/selectors/0.36.1).

## Resumen

| Licencia | Crates de Rust | Paquetes npm |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Apache-2.0 | 380 | 1 |
| MIT | 160 | 14 |
| BSD-3-Clause | 7 | 1 |
| ISC | 6 | 9 |
| Zlib | 3 | 0 |
| Unicode-3.0 | 19 | 0 |
| MPL-2.0 | 5 | 0 |
| CDLA-Permissive-2.0 | 1 | 0 |
| CC0-1.0 | 1 | 0 |

Un crate puede contar en más de una fila cuando su licencia combina varias (por ejemplo `Apache-2.0 AND ISC`).

## Bibliotecas nativas incluidas en el binario

### librdkafka

Incluida a través de `rdkafka-sys 4.10.0+2.12.1`.

```text
// TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A
// PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT
// SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY
// CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR
// IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER
// DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.

pub fn escape(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut quoted = String::new();
    escape_into(text, &mut quoted);
    quoted
}

fn escape_into(text: &str, buf: &mut String) {
    buf.reserve(text.len());
    for c in text.chars() {
        if is_meta_character(c) {
            buf.push('\\');
        }
        buf.push(c);
    }
}

fn is_meta_character(c: char) -> bool {
    match c {
        '\\' | '.' | '+' | '*' | '?' | '(' | ')' | '|' | '[' | ']' | '{' | '}' | '^' | '$'
        | '#' | '&' | '-' | '~' => true,
        _ => false,
    }
}
```

### ISC

Usada por: `rustls-webpki 0.101.7`

```text
// Copyright 2021 Brian Smith.
//
// Permission to use, copy, modify, and/or distribute this software for any
// purpose with or without fee is hereby granted, provided that the above
// copyright notice and this permission notice appear in all copies.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS" AND THE AUTHORS DISCLAIM ALL WARRANTIES
// WITH REGARD TO THIS SOFTWARE INCLUDING ALL IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF
// MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS BE LIABLE FOR
// ANY SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
// WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN
// ACTION OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF
// OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

#[test]
fn cert_without_extensions_test() {
    // Check the certificate is valid with
    // `openssl x509 -in cert_without_extensions.der -inform DER -text -noout`
    const CERT_WITHOUT_EXTENSIONS_DER: &[u8] = include_bytes!("cert_without_extensions.der");

    assert!(webpki::EndEntityCert::try_from(CERT_WITHOUT_EXTENSIONS_DER).is_ok());
}
```

### ISC

Usada por: `rustls-webpki 0.101.7`

```text
// Copyright 2021 Brian Smith.
//
// Permission to use, copy, modify, and/or distribute this software for any
// purpose with or without fee is hereby granted, provided that the above
// copyright notice and this permission notice appear in all copies.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS" AND THE AUTHORS DISCLAIM ALL WARRANTIES
// WITH REGARD TO THIS SOFTWARE INCLUDING ALL IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF
// MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS BE LIABLE FOR
// ANY SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
// WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN
// ACTION OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF
// OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

#[test]
fn test_cert_v1_unsupported() {
    // Check with `openssl x509 -text -noout -in cert_v1.der -inform DER`
    // to verify this is a correct version 1 certificate.
    const CERT_V1_DER: &[u8] = include_bytes!("cert_v1.der");

    assert_eq!(
        Some(webpki::Error::UnsupportedCertVersion),
        webpki::EndEntityCert::try_from(CERT_V1_DER).err()
    );
}
```
